일기토 · 설전 연출 그림 프롬프트

대결 화면 가운데(두 손 아래 층)에 띄울 결과 연출 그림 — 장면 6 × 일기토 · 설전, 디폴트 · 공용 둘(책사 · 노장) + 전용 21명 (화웅 · 사마의는 뽑아 보고 접었다). Gemini용 (2026-11-06)
설전 둘째 묶음(2026-11-06) — 노장 공용 · 노숙 · 육손 · 손권 · 사마의 · 순욱 · 곽가 · 가후 · 장료 · 방통 · 유비 · 이명. 노숙 · 육손 · 사마의 · 순욱 · 곽가 · 가후 · 방통은 설전만. 갑옷 차림 무인은 디폴트가 맡는다 — 설전 그림이 없는 장수는 디폴트를 쓴다.

1. 지난 세 장이 엉망이었던 까닭

  1. 여섯 칸을 한 장에 그리게 했다. 칸이 늘수록 모델이 「누가 왼쪽인가」를 잊는다 — 여포_조운은 왼쪽이 여포로 바뀌었고, 여포_장비의 3번째 칸은 오른쪽 사람이 장비의 사모(뱀창)를 들었다. 5번째 칸은 셋 다 구도가 제멋대로였다.
  2. 두 사람을 다 특정 인물로 적었다. 두 얼굴 · 두 무기를 동시에 지키는 것이 가장 어렵다. 게다가 우리 게임에서는 전용 장수가 아무 상대와 붙어도 같은 그림을 쓴다 → 상대는 얼굴 없는 「아무개 장수」여야 맞다.
  3. 장면의 뜻이 그림으로 안 읽혔다. 「공격권을 가짐」은 말이지 모습이 아니다. 「왼쪽이 내려치고, 오른쪽이 창을 가로로 들어 막으며 말이 뒤로 밀린다」처럼 몸짓으로 적어야 한다.
  4. 화풍이 흔들렸다. 여포_조운은 보라 · 청색 번짐과 HDR 광택이 끼었다. 「먹 한 가지 색 + 피만 붉게」를 매번 적어야 한다.

2. 이렇게 만든다 (작업 순서)

  1. 한 장수 = 새 채팅 하나. 앞 장수의 그림이 섞이지 않는다.
  2. 한 번에 한 장면, 정사각형(1:1) 한 장. 「6칸짜리 한 장」을 시키지 않는다. 자르는 일도 없어진다.
  3. 첫 메시지에 그림 둘을 붙인다 — ① 그 장수의 수묵 초상(assets/CharsInBattle/{이름}.jpg, 아래에 보인다), ② 화풍 기준 그림 한 장(지난 결과 중 마음에 든 칸 하나 — 예: 여포_관우의 1번 칸을 잘라서). 그리고 장면 1 프롬프트.
  4. 장면 1이 마음에 들 때까지 다시 뽑는다. 마음에 든 장면 1을 장면 2 ~ 6을 시킬 때마다 함께 붙인다(「같은 사람 · 같은 화풍으로」의 기준이 된다).
  5. 여섯 장을 아래 파일 이름으로 저장해 assets/duel/scene/에 넣어 주면 굽는 도구(가장자리 흐리기 · 크기)와 화면은 내가 붙인다.

왼쪽 = 이 그림의 주인공(그 전용 장수)이다. 그 장수가 화면 오른쪽에 서는 판에서는 게임이 그림을 좌우로 뒤집고 장면 뜻을 맞바꿔 쓴다(2↔3, 4↔5) — 그러니 언제나 왼쪽이 주인공인 그림만 만들면 된다.

3. 장면 여섯

#일기토설전게임에서 뜨는 때
1첫 격돌 — 무기가 한가운데서 부딪힌다첫 논쟁 — 말이 먹 소용돌이로 맞부딪힌다첫 합 · 비긴 합
2주인공이 몰아붙인다주인공이 몰아붙인다주인공이 공격권을 쥔 합
3상대가 몰아붙인다상대가 몰아붙인다상대가 공격권을 쥔 합
4주인공이 지고 물러난다주인공이 말문이 막혀 자리를 뜬다주인공의 패배 · 도망
5주인공이 이긴다주인공이 이긴다주인공의 승리
6지쳐 떨어져 마주 선다찻잔을 사이에 두고 말이 없다10합 결판 없음

4. 프롬프트

종류와 장수를 누르면 여섯 장면의 프롬프트가 바뀐다(아래 5번 표의 이름을 눌러도 된다). 영어로 적었다 — 그림 모델은 영어 지시를 더 잘 지킨다. [복사]로 그대로 붙여 넣는다.

?
아무개 장수(전용이 없는 모두) — 일기토
첨부: 화풍 기준 그림 한 장만. 디폴트는 전용 그림이 없는 모든 장수에게 쓰인다 — 얼굴이 안 읽혀야 한다.
저장 이름: duel_default_1.png ~ _6.png
1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_default_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): an ANONYMOUS allied general: open-face iron helmet with a short dark plume, his face mostly in shadow under the helmet brim so it reads as "anyone", plain lamellar armor; a plain long spear, riding a DARK horse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the attached image shows the drawing style only.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): LADY ZHURONG: a young, bold, confident WOMAN warrior of the southern tribes; hair in a high bun with a flower ornament and dangling hair pins, a bright fearless smile, light scaled armor over a fitted tunic, earrings; a spear decorated with feathers below the blade and a row of small throwing knives at her belt (she can be throwing one), riding a DARK horse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_je-gal-ryang_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): ZHUGE LIANG: a calm, wise strategist with a thin goatee and mustache, a tall ribbed scholar's hat (lunjin), a layered white scholar's robe with wide sleeves; NO real weapon: he holds his WHITE FEATHER FAN, and with a sweep of the fan a gust of ink-wind and leaves strikes the enemy; he rides a small four-wheeled wooden carriage instead of a horse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): an ANONYMOUS OLD veteran general: a long flowing WHITE beard and white eyebrows clearly visible, deep wrinkles, an open-face iron helmet with a short plume so his eyes are in shadow and the face reads as "any old general", heavy scaled armor, still broad-shouldered and strong; a long spear with a black horsehair tassel below the blade (NO bow, NO quiver, NO saber), riding a DARK warhorse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the attached image shows the drawing style only.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

저장 이름: duel_heo-jeo_1.png ~ _6.png
1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_heo-jeo_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): XU CHU: a HUGE, massively built, barrel-chested warrior, much bulkier than an ordinary man; a topknot bound with a dark cloth HEADBAND tied at the back, a very thick full black beard, fierce scowling eyes under heavy brows, heavy scaled armor with large rounded pauldrons; a huge heavy broad-bladed great saber held in one massive fist, riding a big DARK warhorse that looks strained under his weight.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_seo-hwang_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): XU HUANG: a tall, solidly built, disciplined veteran general in a cape over ornately engraved scaled armor; a round iron helmet with a tall plume and small WING-shaped cheek guards; a downturned mustache and pointed goatee, a stern frown; a great double-bladed battle AXE on a long haft (broad crescent blades on both sides), riding a DARK warhorse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

The whole image reads "LEFT pushes RIGHT back".

이명 (여장군) — 일기토
첨부: assets/CharsInBattle/이명.jpg + 화풍 기준 그림 + (장면 2부터) 마음에 든 장면 1
저장 이름: duel_i-myeong_1.png ~ _6.png
1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_i-myeong_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): LADY LI MING: a calm, graceful young WOMAN warrior, a hermit of Mount Tai; her hair in an elegant high bun with an ornate openwork hair crown and dangling hair ornaments, earrings, a scarf knotted at her throat, light scaled armor; a slim spear, riding a DARK horse; she rides with quiet, wild-animal-like poise.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the first attached image is her portrait — use it ONLY for her face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw her full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: A stalemate after a long, even fight. The two riders have separated and stand apart facing each other across the dusty field at dusk, horses panting, weapons lowered but still alert. Calm, exhausted, mutual respect. Faint banners of both armies far behind.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the first attached image is her portrait — use it ONLY for her face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw her full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. 1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_mun-chu_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): WEN CHOU: a savage warrior with WILD, SHAGGY, DREADLOCK-LIKE black hair spiking out in all directions around a metal-plated headband, a short rough beard, a fierce stare, plain steel plate armor over chain mail; a heavy single-bladed battle AXE on a long haft, riding a DARK warhorse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): ZHANG HE: a sharp-eyed, elegant general with a neat mustache; a helmet whose front is shaped like a snarling LION-DOG (beast) face, with a long flowing plume; finely scaled armor with a cape; a long slender spear with a small red tassel, riding a DARK warhorse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: A stalemate after a long, even fight. The two riders have separated and stand apart facing each other across the dusty field at dusk, horses panting, weapons lowered but still alert. Calm, exhausted, mutual respect. Faint banners of both armies far behind.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.
방덕 — 일기토
첨부: assets/CharsInBattle/방덕.jpg + 화풍 기준 그림 + (장면 2부터) 마음에 든 장면 1
저장 이름: duel_bang-deok_1.png ~ _6.png
1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_bang-deok_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): PANG DE: a stern, battle-hardened general with a full thick beard, wearing a large wrapped TURBAN with a jewel and a feather at the front, heavy scaled armor; a huge broad curved GLAIVE (a long-handled blade with a wide curved head), riding a DARK warhorse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

1. 첫 격돌 / 비김 duel_yu-bi_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: A mounted duel on an open battlefield; dusty ground, faint army banners far in the background.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): LIU BEI: a benevolent, dignified lord with a kind but determined face, a neat mustache and a small goatee; a small ornate crown-cap on his topknot; a layered noble robe with a patterned collar worn over light armor; TWO swords crossed on his back; TWIN straight swords (one in each hand, a matched pair of double-edged jian), riding a DARK horse.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS enemy general on a PALE GREY horse. Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The first clash. Both riders charge at full gallop toward each other and their weapons collide exactly in the center of the image with a burst of ink sparks. Both horses rear. Perfectly even footing, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only his own weapon.

Closed iron helmet with a face guard and cheek flaps so his face cannot be seen, plain dark lamellar armor, a plain straight spear. He must not resemble any famous character: no beard showing, no feathers, no plume, no special weapon.

SCENE: The LEFT rider has won. In the left foreground he sits tall and proud on his horse, raising his weapon high in triumph. On the right the defeated anonymous general slumps in the saddle and his horse turns away, fleeing toward the RIGHT edge; a small dark-red blood stain on the loser.

He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): GUAN YU: tall and imposing, a VERY long flowing black beard reaching his chest, narrow stern eyes, dark cloth head wrap, a long war robe over his armor; in his long war robe, stroking his long beard with one hand, calm and dignified.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The LEFT man has lost the debate. Head lowered, shoulders slumped, he turns away and walks out of the hall toward the LEFT edge. The rival stands composed on the right; officials in the background hide their laughter behind their sleeves.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

1. 첫 논쟁 / 비김 debate_jang-bi_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): ZHANG FEI: a burly, wild-looking warrior with a thick bushy black beard, fierce round bulging eyes, a cloth head wrap with a square metal plate at the front, heavy scaled armor; in armor, slamming the table with his fist, shouting (still no text).

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): ZHAO YUN: a youthful, calm, handsome general; a bright silver ornate helmet with a long WHITE plume flowing behind; finely scaled silver armor with a white cape; in his silver armor and white cape, standing straight, speaking calmly.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The LEFT man has lost the debate. Head lowered, shoulders slumped, he turns away and walks out of the hall toward the LEFT edge. The rival stands composed on the right; officials in the background hide their laughter behind their sleeves.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

저장 이름: debate_ma-cho_1.png ~ _6.png
1. 첫 논쟁 / 비김 debate_ma-cho_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): MA CHAO: a young, fierce, handsome general wearing a LION-HEAD helmet (the lion's open jaws frame his face) with a long dark plume, lion-face pauldrons on both shoulders, ornate scaled armor; in the lion-head helmet and armor, glaring, chin raised.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

1. 첫 논쟁 / 비김 debate_jo-jo_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.

Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): CAO CAO: a shrewd, commanding ruler in his forties with a neat short beard and mustache, piercing eyes, a small ornate crown-cap on his topknot, ornate engraved armor with a cape; in a rich court robe with his crown-cap, half-smiling, eyes narrowed, one hand raised in a dismissive gesture.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

1. 첫 논쟁 / 비김 debate_ju-yu_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): ZHOU YU: an elegant, very handsome young commander with LONG flowing hair loose over his shoulders, a confident slight smile, ornate scaled armor; in a fine long robe over light armor, long hair flowing, a slight confident smile.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. 책사 공용 (말을 탄 책사 — 전용이 없는 책사 모두) — 설전 그림은 안 만든다(설전에서는 디폴트를 쓴다).
?
노장 공용 (흰 수염 노장 — 전용이 없는 노장 모두) — 설전
첨부: 화풍 기준 그림 + (장면 2부터) 마음에 든 장면 1. 공용 한 벌이라 초상은 안 붙인다 — 여러 장수가 함께 쓰므로 특정 얼굴이 읽히면 안 된다.

STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): LU XUN: a very YOUNG, beardless, sharp-browed scholar-general with an intense, serious stare; his hair pulled back in a high knot with several thin BRAIDS falling beside his face; a plain scholar's robe with an ornate belt; gripping a tall plain wooden STAFF hung with a decorative KNOTTED TASSEL, standing very straight, quiet cold resolve in his eyes; NO fan.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

SCENE: The LEFT man is on the offensive. He steps forward and points straight at the rival; a powerful wave of swirling ink strokes flows from LEFT to RIGHT and crashes over the rival. The rival leans back, sweating, raising his sleeve to shield his face. The officials in the background turn to look at the left man in admiration.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

사마의 — 설전
첨부: assets/CharsInBattle/사마의.jpg + 화풍 기준 그림 + (장면 2부터) 마음에 든 장면 1
저장 이름: debate_sa-ma-ui_1.png ~ _6.png
1. 첫 논쟁 / 비김 debate_sa-ma-ui_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): SIMA YI: a calm, cunning, hooded-eyed statesman with a thin mustache and a long narrow goatee; a TALL ridged official's cap pierced by a long horizontal HAIRPIN from which two long WHITE RIBBONS hang down on both sides; a richly patterned dark court robe; one hand resting on the hilt of a sword at his waist, the other hidden in his sleeve, a faint, patient, unreadable smile — a man who waits; NO fan.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): SIMA YI: a calm, cunning, hooded-eyed statesman with a thin mustache and a long narrow goatee; a TALL ridged official's cap pierced by a long horizontal HAIRPIN from which two long WHITE RIBBONS hang down on both sides; a richly patterned dark court robe; one hand resting on the hilt of a sword at his waist, the other hidden in his sleeve, a faint, patient, unreadable smile — a man who waits; NO fan.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: A stalemate. Both men are seated on either side of the low table with cups of tea between them, silent, regarding each other with wary respect. Evening light falls through the lattice windows.

1. 첫 논쟁 / 비김 debate_sun-uk_1.png
STYLE: Traditional Chinese ink-wash illustration with a gritty manga line style. Monochrome only: black and charcoal-grey ink on warm cream rice paper, dry-brush texture, ink splatter and speed strokes. The ONLY color allowed is a few small touches of dark red for blood. No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): XUN YU: a dignified, upright, handsome court minister with a neat mustache and a full pointed beard; a TALL, ornately patterned official's crown-cap with a jewel at the front; a heavily PATTERNED court robe with swirling cloud motifs; holding a DARK, large-feathered fan (NOT white) against his chest, standing formally, a principled, measured, unshakable calm.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: A stalemate. Both men are seated on either side of the low table with cups of tea between them, silent, regarding each other with wary respect. Evening light falls through the lattice windows.

He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The opening exchange. Both men stand facing each other across a low table, both gesturing sharply. Their words are drawn as swirling ink-brush streams (abstract strokes, NO readable characters) that collide in the center of the image in a burst of ink. Perfectly even, neither is winning.

No purple, no blue, no colored tint, no glossy HDR lighting, no 3D render look.
PROPORTIONS: realistic adult human anatomy for BOTH people — the head is about one seventh of the body height, both shown full-body at the same scale. NOT chibi, NOT super-deformed, NOT big-headed, NOT a close-up bust.
FORMAT: ONE single square illustration (1:1), one moment only. NOT a comic page: no panels, no grid, no border, no frame. No text, no letters, no calligraphy characters, no seal stamps, no signature, no watermark. Keep the action inside the middle 70% of the square; let the outer edges fade into plain empty rice paper (the game will fade the edges out).

PLACE: Inside a grand palace council hall: tall wooden pillars, lattice windows, low lacquered desks, rows of seated court officials in the background (small, blurred).

LEFT side, facing right (the MAIN character of this image): PANG TONG: a stout, round-faced, plain-looking strategist with heavy cheeks, a thin mustache and a scruffy goatee; a small simple scholar's cap tied with a long loose cloth RIBBON fluttering behind; a plain patterned robe; a thick ROLLED SCROLL tucked under his arm, eyes squeezed into a broad, cheerful, knowing grin, relaxed and unbothered; NO fan.

RIGHT side, facing left: an ANONYMOUS rival strategist in a plain dark scholar's robe and a simple black cloth cap, his face turned three-quarters away or half hidden behind a raised sleeve, holding a bamboo scroll. He must not resemble any famous character: no feather fan, no distinctive hat, no long beard.

SCENE: The LEFT man has won. He stands calm and upright with a faint smile. On the right the rival bows deeply in defeat, his bamboo scroll dropped on the floor. The officials in the background bow toward the left man.

Keep the LEFT person on the left and the RIGHT person on the right; each holds only their own item.

Reference: the first attached image is his portrait — use it ONLY for his face, hair, headwear and gear; it is a close-up bust, so do NOT copy its framing or head size — draw him full-body at normal adult proportions, the same scale as the person on the right. The other attached image shows the drawing style.

AVOID: big heads or chibi proportions; swapping the two people's sides or weapons; a third person in the foreground; extra hands or extra weapons; the RIGHT-side person looking like any famous character; any text.

6. 설전의 패 셋 — 명분 · 실리 · 대세 (2026-11-07)

설전은 「상대가 기댄 논점을 찌르는」 놀이다 — 규칙은 그대로(셋 중 하나 맞히기), 옛 산가지 1 · 2 · 3 그림만 바꾼다. 일기토의 손 셋(ic_rps_*)과 같은 결로: 검은 바탕 · 물건 하나 · 금빛 번짐, 글자는 넣지 않는다(모양과 색으로 읽혀야 한다 — 그림 모델의 한자는 틀리기 쉽다). 받은 그림은 적힌 이름 그대로 assets/icons/ui/control/_src/에 덮어 두고 npm run duel.

1. 명분(名分) assets/icons/ui/control/_src/ic_debate_1.png.jpg
A single game UI icon in a hand-painted, semi-realistic fantasy RPG icon style: rich warm colors, soft painterly shading, a clean readable silhouette with a thin dark outline and a soft warm golden glow hugging the edge.
ONE object only, centered, filling about 60% of the frame height, slightly tilted for life.
BACKGROUND: pure flat black (#000000) — no gradient, no floor, no cast shadow, no smoke.
Wide 16:9 canvas. Ancient China, Three Kingdoms era.

MEANING: CAUSE — legitimacy, the righteous claim.

OBJECT: an ancient imperial JADE SEAL: a square block of glowing green jade topped by a coiled dragon knob, a red silk cord with a golden tassel tied around the knob, the flat seal face turned slightly toward the viewer and stained with red cinnabar ink (plain red square, no characters).

AVOID: any text, letters, Chinese characters or numbers anywhere (the object must be readable by shape and color alone); hands or people; a frame or border; more than one main object; a background scene.
